\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 3. Final Audit Scope and Preservation Rules\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 This final audit covers whether COMMERCIAL-REV is materially responsive to COMMERCIAL-AUDIT-R01\'96R21, materially compliant with COMMERCIAL-SYN-F01\'96F24, and safe to move into Commercial Output Specificity / Non-Regression Test Mapping.\
It does not cover production implementation, code, backend behaviour, schema, UI rendering, comparison-page behaviour, live Commercial output quality, actual prompt deployment, score recalculation code or product rollout.\
The following remain preserved without change:\
\pard\tx220\tx720\pardeftab720\li720\fi-720\sa240\partightenfactor0
\ls2\ilvl0\cf0 \kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 six stored score fields: 
\f2\fs26 technical
\f1\fs24 , 
\f2\fs26 audio
\f1\fs24 , 
\f2\fs26 vocal
\f1\fs24 , 
\f2\fs26 acting
\f1\fs24 , 
\f2\fs26 brief_adherence
\f1\fs24 , 
\f2\fs26 professional_presentation
\f1\fs24 ;\
\ls2\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 current Commercial weighting: acting as presence/naturalism 60%, brief_adherence 20%, technical 15%, audio 5%;\
\ls2\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 score caps, blockers, verdict thresholds and role-fit bounds;\
\ls2\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 server-side score recomputation;\
\ls2\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Step 1 evidence pass and Step 2 text-only polish;\
\ls2\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 locked-field enforcement;\
\ls2\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 report schema and public JSON structure;\
\ls2\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 MT acting + song stabilised flow;\
\ls2\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Dance, Acting and Voice / Singing label protections;\
\ls2\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 safety, material-policy, presentation and accessibility scrubs.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0
\cf0 A return to COMMERCIAL-REV would be required only if the revised text introduced a schema/weight change, failed to clarify Commercial field semantics, allowed marketability/appearance/polish scoring, collapsed Commercial into generic Acting, or failed to block no-brief invention and live-room overclaim. No such blocking failure is present.\

\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 7. Topic-by-Topic Final Audit Summary\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\fs28 \cf0 Commercial distinctiveness from theatre acting\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Status: pass. The revised baseline explicitly blocks theatre-acting default language and redirects feedback to Commercial copy, addressee, tone and task scale. No revision loop required.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Commercial distinctiveness from generic screen acting\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Status: pass. The revised baseline distinguishes Commercial from generic screen acting where copy, product, campaign and situation context matter. No revision loop required.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Acting-as-presence/naturalism field semantics\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Status: pass with limitation. The wording clarifies Commercial 
\f2\fs26 acting
\f1\fs24  as observable presence/naturalism without renaming the field. Structural field-sharing remains a limitation, not a blocker.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Generic \'93natural / confident / presence\'94 suppression\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Status: pass. The revised text names risky phrases and requires behavioural replacement evidence. Output enforcement remains live-QA dependent.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Tone calibration\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Status: pass. Tone must be grounded in brief, copy, product, situation or observable tape evidence. No revision loop required.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Copy handling\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Status: pass. Copy communication, cue-word handling, rhythm and point of view are now named as Commercial evidence where supported. Voiceover use remains conditional.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Product / brand / situation grounding\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Status: pass. The revised baseline blocks unsupported product/brand/audience invention and allows grounding only where supplied or visible.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Direct-to-camera handling\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Status: pass. Direct address and lens relationship are task-specific. No universal direct-to-camera rule is introduced.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Off-camera-reader / reader-scene handling\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Status: pass. Reader-scene work is treated as mediated; reader access remains process rather than talent evidence.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 No-dialogue / silent-vignette Commercial\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Status: pass with limitation. The revision handles no-dialogue work conditionally and does not generalise it.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Voiceover-style Commercial as conditional\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Status: pass with limitation. Voiceover-style evidence is explicitly conditional and low-confidence relative to B1\'96B3.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 UGC / social-style limitation\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Status: pass. The revision keeps UGC/social-style as a limitation, not a universal Commercial subtype.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Presenter-led product limitation\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Status: pass. Presenter-led product remains under-evidenced and safely constrained.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Corporate / industrial limitation\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Status: pass. Corporate/industrial remains an explicit limitation.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 No-brief brand / product / situation invention\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Status: pass. No-brief Commercial reports are restricted to observable evidence. This is a critical non-regression target.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Technical assessability versus performance\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Status: pass. Assessability is a precondition and limited sight/sound narrows reliability before performance criticism.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Audio / speech audibility\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Status: pass. Poor audio does not prove weak Commercial performance or weak speech skill.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Simple capture / anti-polish\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Status: pass. Domestic capture and phones are protected; studio polish and production value are blocked as merit.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Equipment / production-value exclusion\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Status: pass. Expensive equipment, studio polish, paid coaching and paid editing are excluded as talent evidence.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Reader-as-process support\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Status: pass. Reader access, quality and paid-resource status are process issues.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Self-tape burden, upload and deadline process boundaries\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Status: pass. Early submission, upload friction, file naming and deadline pressure are bounded as process/admin.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 professional_presentation boundaries\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Status: pass. professional_presentation is limited to assessability, brief response, safe process and observable task preparation.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 brief_adherence / professional standards boundaries\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Status: pass. Explicit brief compliance remains possible, but no-brief invention is blocked.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Live-room-only / process-only overclaim\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Status: pass. Direction response, callback readiness and client-room behaviour are blocked unless directly shown.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Callback readiness and response-to-direction scope\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Status: pass. Finished tape evidence cannot prove these capacities unless explicitly present.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Nudity / stunt / privacy / consent boundaries\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Status: pass. These remain safety/process issues, not performer merit or willingness.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Accessibility-safe and non-deficit handling\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Status: pass with limitation. Strong non-deficit rule added; accent, speech difference and gender-diverse voice detail remains broad.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Protected-characteristic and stereotype-led suitability boundaries\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Status: pass. The revision blocks protected-trait and stereotype-led suitability claims.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Marketability / bookability / appearance exclusion\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Status: pass with limitation. The exclusion is strong enough for wording-level approval; some support is inferential rather than verbatim Commercial-specific.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Methodology / practitioner / source-shape overreach\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Status: pass. Source-shape caution is explicit and B4 is correctly conditional.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Display / label / comparison risks\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Status: accepted with limitation. Display behaviour remains unverified and is correctly carried forward.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Timestamp and next-take specificity\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Status: pass with limitation. Commercial-specific timestamp and next-take anchors are now present; renderer/export behaviour remains unverified.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa298\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 8. Preservation and Non-Regression Audit\

\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 12. Final Release Decision\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\fs24 \cf0 COMMERCIAL-FINAL-D01 \'97 Approved with explicit limitations
\f1\b0 \
The revised Commercial baseline is materially responsive to COMMERCIAL-AUDIT-R01\'96R21 and materially aligned with COMMERCIAL-SYN-F01\'96F24. It is approved at wording, evidence-standard and guardrail level.\
There are no critical blockers requiring another COMMERCIAL-REV pass. The revised package:\
\pard\tx220\tx720\pardeftab720\li720\fi-720\sa240\partightenfactor0
\ls3\ilvl0\cf0 \kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 clarifies Commercial 
\f2\fs26 acting
\f1\fs24  as presence/naturalism through observable Commercial evidence;\
\ls3\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 prevents theatre-acting and generic screen-acting leakage;\
\ls3\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 grounds tone, copy, product and situation claims;\
\ls3\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 blocks no-brief brand/product/audience invention;\
\ls3\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 splits direct-to-camera and reader-scene Commercial handling;\
\ls3\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 adds conditional no-dialogue and voiceover-style handling;\
\ls3\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 carries weak subtypes as limitations;\
\ls3\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 separates assessability, audio and technical quality from performance;\
\ls3\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 protects simple capture and blocks production polish;\
\ls3\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 treats reader/admin/deadline burdens as process-only;\
\ls3\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 blocks live-room and callback overclaim;\
\ls3\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 strengthens accessibility, anti-deficit, anti-marketability and anti-appearance rules;\
\ls3\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 preserves all protected shared-system constraints.\

\f0\b \cf0 COMMERCIAL-FINAL-D02 \'97 Approved for Commercial Output Specificity / Non-Regression Test Mapping with explicit limitation set
\f1\b0 \
COMMERCIAL-OUTPUT-SPEC can proceed. It must carry forward the following limitations:\

\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 UGC/social, presenter-led product and corporate/industrial evidence thin;\

\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 voiceover-style evidence conditional only;\

\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 marketability/appearance exclusion partly inferential but strongly supported by fairness guardrails;\
\ls4\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 accent, speech difference and gender-diverse voice detail broad;\
\ls4\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0

\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 13. Reusable Handoff Pack for COMMERCIAL-OUTPUT-SPEC\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 COMMERCIAL-FINAL-AUDIT is complete. The COMMERCIAL-REV package is approved with explicit limitations and is ready for Commercial Output Specificity / Non-Regression Test Mapping. The revision remains inside the live TapeCoach shared architecture and does not propose any score-field, weighting, cap, blocker, verdict-threshold, role-fit, schema, backend, pipeline, report-schema or UI implementation changes.\
The strongest accepted changes are COMMERCIAL-REV-C01 to C06 and C10 to C18. These clarify Commercial distinctiveness, 
\f2\fs26 acting
\f1\fs24  as Commercial presence/naturalism, copy/tone/product grounding, no-brief restraint, task-specific camera handling, assessability, audio reliability, anti-polish, process-only reader/admin/deadline boundaries, live-room claim limits, safety, accessibility, anti-marketability and professional_presentation. COMMERCIAL-REV-C03 and C20 also provide strong anti-generic and timestamp/next-take specificity rules.\
Accepted with limitation: COMMERCIAL-REV-C07, C08, C09, C16, C20 and C21. The limitations are evidence or verification limits, not revision blockers. No-dialogue evidence is thinner than mainstream Commercial evidence. Voiceover-style evidence is conditional and comes mainly from B4. UGC/social, presenter-led and corporate/industrial subtypes remain under-evidenced. Accessibility detail for accent, speech difference and gender-diverse voice remains broad. Display labels, comparison behaviour and renderer/export timestamp behaviour were not auditable.\
Final accepted section set: COMMERCIAL-REV-S01\'96S21, with limitations attached to S07, S08, S09, S16, S20 and S21.\
The next stage should convert the revised package into Commercial output-specificity rules, adversarial scenarios, display-layer checks and non-regression tests. Highest-priority output-spec scenarios are direct-to-camera Commercial, reader-scene Commercial, no-brief Commercial, supplied brand/product/copy, generic \'93natural/confident\'94 wording, poor lighting/framing, poor audio, simple home capture, high polish with weak performance, paid reader, response-to-direction overclaim, callback readiness overclaim, no-brief brand/product/audience invention, marketability/bookability/look language, access-adapted context, speech/accent/gender-diverse voice safety, production polish in professional_presentation, and timestamp underproduction.\
Evidence basis: COMMERCIAL-SYN-F01\'96F24, COMMERCIAL-AUDIT-I01\'96I17, COMMERCIAL-AUDIT-R01\'96R21, COMMERCIAL-REV-C01\'96C21, COMMERCIAL-REV-S01\'96S21, COMMERCIAL-S001\'96S030, COMMERCIAL-B1-F01\'96F09, COMMERCIAL-B2-F01\'96F09, COMMERCIAL-B3-F01\'96F10 and COMMERCIAL-B4-F01\'96F06.\
What was auditable: revised Commercial wording, audit recommendation response, synthesis compliance and baseline non-regression posture.\
What was not auditable: exact production prompt text, frontend labels, comparison-page labels, renderer/export timestamp behaviour, live Commercial output quality and implementation-layer subtype detection.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa298\partightenfactor0
